# India - PMO archive (archivepmo.nic.in), article URLs for ind_archivepmo.yml.
#
# TWO former-PM subtrees on one host:
#   /drmanmohansingh/  Manmohan Singh, 22 May 2004 - 26 May 2014
#   /abv/              Atal Bihari Vajpayee
# SIX article endpoints are taken (CLAUDE.md RULE 1 - the cleaner sifts, not the harvest):
#   speech-details, press-details, interview-details, pmsinparliament,
#   pmmessageappeals, pmvisit_list
# Deliberately EXCLUDED: content_print.php (print-view duplicates of pages already
# taken, 3,753 captures), photogallery*/getdoc (galleries and binaries), and every
# ln=hindi variant (2,449 captures) - this recipe is source_language: English.
#
# 4479 unique articles, derived from the Internet Archive CDX index of the host on
# 2026-09-01 (14,415 captures). The Archive is used as an INDEX only - every fetch goes to
# the LIVE site, which is up and serves all six endpoints. The live listings cannot be used
# for enumeration: speeches.php and its siblings show only the 10 most recent items and
# ignore every pagination parameter tried (pageid/page/pn/start all return page 1).
#
